services with oceanic culture as its core. The Group owned and      文旅集團。集團在全國範圍內擁有並運
operated seven large-scale marine culture-based theme parks         營7家以「海昌」為品牌、以海洋文化為主
under the brand name of “Haichang” in China. While improving        題的大型主題公園。在提升主題公園遊
visitors’ experience and product offerings in theme parks, the      客體驗和產品的同時，集團逐漸將業務
Group gradually diversified its business to tourism and leisure     拓展至文旅服務及解決方案業務、IP運營
services and solutions, and IP operation new consumption. As        新消費業務，通過持續的將我們的文旅
the Group continues to deliver its comprehensive tourism and        綜合資源和優秀的IP零售產品輸出到全國
leisure resources and premium IP merchandises across China,         各地，本集團讓更多的消費者能夠充分
more consumers are able to fully enjoy the Group’s wide range       享受到我們豐富且高質量的休閒文旅消
of high-quality culture-based tourism and leisure consumer          費產品。本集團的目標是成為一家讓大
products. The goal of the Group is to become a company helping      家玩得更好的公司，希望能打造一個「有

     CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                        企業管治常規
     The Group is committed to maintaining high standards of               本集團致力維持高水平的企業管治，以
     corporate governance to safeguard the interests of Shareholders       保障股東利益及提升企業價值和問責
     and to enhance corporate value and accountability. The                性。本公司已採納上市規則附錄C1 所載
     Company has adopted the code provisions in Part 2 of Corporate        的企業管治守則（「企業管治守則」）第二
     Governance Code (the “CG Code”) contained in Appendix C1 to           部分守則條文作為其本身的企業管治守
     the Listing Rules as its own code of corporate governance.            則。


     As at the date of this annual report, the Company is in deviation     截至本年報日期，本公司違反企業管治
     from the following code provisions of the CG Code:                    守則的情況如下：

     CHAIRMAN AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE OFFICER                                  主席及行政總裁
     As required by code provision C.2.1 of the CG Code, the roles         根據企業管治守則的守則條文第C.2.1
     of chairman and chief executive officer should be separate            條，主席與行政總裁的角色應予區分，
     and performed by different individuals. Mr. Qu Naijie acts as         不應由同一人兼任。曲乃杰先生擔任本
     the chairman of the Board and the chief executive officer of          公司董事會主席兼行政總裁。本公司認
     the Company. The Company considers that it is appropriate             為偏離該規定實屬恰當，原因為由同一
     to deviate from the code provision as taking the roles of both        人兼任主席和行政總裁角色有助提高制
     chairman and chief executive by the same individual helps             定及執行本公司策略之效率，讓本集團
     enhance the efficiency of the formulation and implementation of       更高效及時地抓緊商機。本公司認為，
     the Company’s strategies and allow the Group to grasp business        在董事會及獨立非執行董事之監督下，
     opportunities in an efficient and timely manner. The Company          制衡機制可充分及公平呈現股東利益。
     is of the view that under the supervision of the Board and the
     independent non-executive Directors, a balancing mechanism
     exists so that the interests of the Shareholders are adequately and
     fairly represented.

     The chairman of the Board and chief executive officer of the          董事會主席兼本公司行政總裁負責制定
     Company is responsible for overall strategic planning and             本集團整體策略，並監督一般企業、財
     overseeing the general corporate, financial and compliance            務及合規事宜，以及戰略性決策及本集
     affairs of the Group, and the strategic decision making and the       團管理。

     BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY                                                   董事會成員多元化政策
     The Company believes that the increasing diversity at the Board          本公司相信，董事會成員日益多樣化乃
     level is one of the essential elements in supporting the attainment      支持其實現戰略目標及可持續發展的基
     of its strategic objectives and its sustainable development.             本要素之一。因此，本公司已根據企業
     Therefore, the Company has adopted a board diversity policy (the         管治守則所載規定採納董事會成員多元
     “Board Diversity Policy”) in accordance with the requirement             化政策（「董事會成員多元化政策」）。董
     set out in the CG Code. Diversity of Board members can be                事會成員多元化可透過考慮多項因素而
     achieved through consideration of a number of factors, including         達致，其中除教育背景、專業經驗、技
     but not limited to gender, age, cultural background and ethnicity,       能、知識及服務年期外，亦包括（但不限
     in addition to educational background, professional experience,          於）性別、年齡、文化背景及種族。董事
     skills, knowledge and length of service. All Board appointments          會所有委任均以用人唯才為原則，並在
     will be based on meritocracy, and candidates will be considered          考慮人選時以適當的條件充分顧及董事
     against appropriate criteria, having due regard for the benefits of      會成員多元化的裨益。
     diversity on the Board.

      give the appearance of being involved in corruption or unethical        牽涉入賄賂及不道德商業行為之情況，

       Key audit matter                                             How our audit addressed the key audit matter
       關鍵審核事項                                                       關鍵審核事項在審核中的處理方法

       The impairment testing was important to our audit
       due to the significance of the carrying amount of the
       assets for operation (69.49% of the total assets as
       at 31 December 2023) and the judgement involved
       in determining key assumptions such as future
       sales growth, profit margins and discount rates, in
       particular for theme parks with an aggregate carrying
       amount of RMB6,654,690,000 as at 31 December
       2023 which incurred losses.


       鑒於營運資產賬面值的重大性（於2023 年12 月31 日佔
       總資產的69.49%）及在釐定未來銷售額增長、利潤率
       及折現率等主要假設過程中所涉及的判斷，該減值測試
       對吾等的審核工作意義重大，尤其是，於2023 年12月
       31日賬面總值為人民幣6,654,690,000元的主題公園錄
       得虧損。


       Relevant disclosures are included in notes 4 and 12
       to the consolidated financial statements.


       相關披露載於合併財務報表附註4及12。

                                                                                   權。

3.   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                      3. 重大會計政策
     Investment in an associate                                            於聯營公司的投資
     An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long-term          聯營公司為本集團擁有股本表決權
     interest of generally not less than 20% of the equity voting          整體不少於20%的長期權益且具有
     rights and over which it has significant influence. Significant       重大影響力的實體。重大影響力為
     influence is the power to participate in the financial and            參與投資對象財務及經營政策決定
     operating policy decisions of the investee, but is not control        的權力，但並非該等政策的控制權
     or joint control over those policies.                                 或共同控制權。

     The Group’s investment in an associate is stated in the               本集團於聯營公司的投資根據權益
     consolidated statement of financial position at the Group’s           會計法，按本集團分佔的淨資產減
     share of net assets under the equity method of accounting,            任何減值虧損，於合併財務狀況表
     less any impairment losses. The Group’s share of the post-            中列賬。本集團分佔聯營公司的收
     acquisition results and other comprehensive income of the             購後業績及其他全面收益，分別計
     associate is included in the consolidated statement of profit         入合併損益及合併其他全面收益表
     or loss and consolidated other comprehensive income,                  內。此外，如有直接確認於聯營公
     respectively. In addition, when there has been a change               司權益的變動，本集團於適當情況
     recognised directly in the equity of the associate, the Group         下在合併權益變動表中確認其分佔
     recognises its share of any changes, when applicable, in              的任何變動。本集團與其聯營公司
     the consolidated statement of changes in equity. Unrealised           之間的交易所產生的未變現收益及
     gains and losses resulting from transactions between the              虧損予以對銷，以本集團於該聯營
     Group and its associate are eliminated to the extent of               公司的投資為限，惟倘未變現虧損
     the Group’s investment in the associate, except where                 證明所轉讓資產出現減值則除外。
     unrealised losses provide evidence of an impairment of the            由收購聯營公司產生的商譽計入為
     assets transferred. Goodwill arising from the acquisition of          本集團於聯營公司的投資一部分。
     the associate is included as part of the Group’s investment
     in the associate.

     The Group determines that it has acquired a business                  本集團決定當所取得的一組活動及
     when the acquired set of activities and assets includes an            資產包括一項投入及一項實質性過
     input and a substantive process that together significantly           程，而二者共同顯著促進創造產出
     contribute to the ability to create outputs.                          的能力時，其已收購一項業務。

     When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the                   本集團收購一項業務時會根據合約
     financial assets and liabilities assumed for appropriate              條款、收購日之經濟狀況及有關條
     classification and designation in accordance with the                 件評估取得的金融資產及承擔的金
     contractual terms, economic circumstances and pertinent               融負債，以進行適當分類及指定。
     conditions as at the acquisition date. This includes the              這包括分離被收購方主合約中的嵌
     separation of embedded derivatives in host contracts of the           入式衍生工具。
     acquiree.


     If the business combination is achieved in stages, the                倘業務合併為分階段實現，先前持
     previously held equity interest is remeasured at its                  有的股本權益應按收購日的公允價
     acquisition date fair value and any resulting gain or loss is         值重新計算，產生的任何收益或虧
     recognised in profit or loss.                                         損在損益中確認。

     Any contingent consideration to be transferred by the                 收購方轉讓的任何或有代價將以收
     acquirer is recognised at fair value at the acquisition date.         購日的公允價值確認。被分類為資
     Contingent consideration classified as an asset or liability is       產或負債的或有代價按公允價值確
     measured at fair value with changes in fair value recognised          認，其公允價值的變化於損益確
     in profit or loss. Contingent consideration that is classified        認。或有代價如被分類為權益項
     as equity is not remeasured and subsequent settlement is              目，則不再對其重新計量，後續的
     accounted for within equity.                                          結算會計入權益中。

           The Group measures its investment properties, investment              本集團於各報告期末按公允價值計
           properties classified as held for sale, equity investments            量其投資物業、分類為持作出售的
           and biological assets at fair value at the end of each                投資物業、股本投資及生物資產。
           reporting period. Fair value is the price that would be               公允價值為市場參與者於計量日期
           received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability in          在有序交易中出售資產所收取的價
           an orderly transaction between market participants at the             格或轉讓負債所支付的價格。公允
           measurement date. The fair value measurement is based                 價值計量乃根據假設出售資產或轉
           on the presumption that the transaction to sell the asset or          讓負債的交易於資產或負債主要市
           transfer the liability takes place either in the principal market     場或（在無主要市場情況下）最具優
           for the asset or liability, or in the absence of a principal          勢市場進行而作出。主要或最具優
           market, in the most advantageous market for the asset or              勢市場須為本集團可進入之市場。
           liability. The principal or the most advantageous market              資產或負債的公允價值乃按假設市
           must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset           場參與者於資產或負債定價時會以
           or a liability is measured using the assumptions that market          其最佳經濟利益行事計量。
           participants would use when pricing the asset or liability,
           assuming that market participants act in their economic best
           interest.


           A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into          非金融資產的公允價值計量須計及
           account a market participant’s ability to generate economic           市場參與者能自最大限度使用該資
           benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by         產達致最佳用途，或將該資產出售
           selling it to another market participant that would use the           予將最大限度使用該資產達致最佳
           asset in its highest and best use.                                    用途的其他市場參與者，所產生的
                                                                                 經濟效益。

           The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in           本集團採納適用於不同情況且具備
           the circumstances and for which sufficient data are available         充分數據以供計量公允價值的估值
           to measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant                 方法，以盡量使用相關可觀察輸入
           observable inputs and minimising the use of unobservable              數據及盡量減少使用不可觀察輸入
           inputs.                                                               數據。

           carrying amounts will be recovered principally through a           銷售交易而非透過持續使用收回，
           sales transaction rather than through continuing use. For this     則分類為持作出售。在此情況下，
           to be the case, the asset must be available for immediate          該資產可按當前狀況即時出售，惟
           sale in its present condition subject only to terms that are       僅須受出售該等資產的一般及慣用
           usual and customary for the sale of such assets and its sale       條款所規限，且實現其出售的概率
           must be highly probable.                                           必須極高。

     Investments and other financial assets                                投資及其他金融資產（續）
     (continued)
     Initial recognition and measurement (continued)                       首次確認及計量（續）
     The Group’s business model for managing financial assets              本集團管理金融資產的業務模式，
     refers to how it manages its financial assets in order to             乃指其管理金融資產以產生現金流
     generate cash flows. The business model determines                    量的方法。業務模式決定現金流量
     whether cash flows will result from collecting contractual            會否源於收取合約現金流量、出售
     cash flows, selling the financial assets, or both. Financial          金融資產或同時兩者。按攤餘成本
     assets classified and measured at amortised cost are                  分類及計量的金融資產於一個目的
     held within a business model with the objective to hold               為持有金融資產以收取合約現金流
     financial assets in order to collect contractual cash flows,          量的業務模式中持有，而透過其他
     while financial assets classified and measured at fair value          全面收益按公允價值分類及計量的
     through other comprehensive income are held within a                  金融資產於一個目的同時為持有以
     business model with the objective of both holding to collect          收取合約現金流量及出售的業務模
     contractual cash flows and selling. Financial assets which            式中持有。並非於上述業務模式中
     are not held within the aforementioned business models are            持有的金融資產透過損益按公允價
     classified and measured at fair value through profit or loss.         值分類及計量。

     Purchases or sales of financial assets that require delivery of       購入或售出須於一般由市場規例或
     assets within the period generally established by regulation          慣例指定的期間內交付資產的金融
     or convention in the marketplace are recognised on the                資產於交易日期（即本集團承諾購入
     trade date, that is, the date that the Group commits to               或售出資產的日期）確認。
     purchase or sell the asset.


     Subsequent measurement                                                後續計量
     The subsequent measurement of financial assets depends                不同類別金融資產的後續計量方法
     on their classification as follows:                                   如下：

     Financial assets at amortised cost (debt instruments)                 按攤餘成本計算的金融資產（債務工
                                                                           具）
     Financial assets at amortised cost are subsequently                   按攤餘成本計算的金融資產其後採
     measured using the effective interest method and are                  用實際利率法計量，並可能被減
     subject to impairment. Gains and losses are recognised                值。當有關資產被終止確認、更改
     in the statement of profit or loss when the asset is                  或減值時，收益及虧損在損益表中
     derecognised, modified or impaired.                                   確認。

      3.   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                      3. 重大會計政策（續）
           (continued)
           Impairment of financial assets                                      金融資產減值
           The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit               本集團就所有並非透過損益按公允
           losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value     價值列賬持有的債務工具確認預期
           through profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference            信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）撥備。
           between the contractual cash flows due in accordance with           預期信貸虧損基於根據合約到期的
           the contract and all the cash flows that the Group expects          合約現金流量與本集團預期收取的
           to receive, discounted at an approximation of the original          所有現金流量（按原實際利率的約數
           effective interest rate. The expected cash flows will include       折現）之間的差額計算。預期現金流
           cash flows from the sale of collateral held or other credit         量將包括來自出售所持有屬於合約
           enhancements that are integral to the contractual terms.            條款整體部分的抵押品或其他信貸
                                                                               加強措施的現金流量。

           General approach                                                   一般方法
           ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures             預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。對
           for which there has not been a significant increase in credit       於自首次確認以來信貸風險並無大
           risk since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit        幅增加的信貸敞口而言，就因未來
           losses that result from default events that are possible within     12 個月內可能發生的違約事件而引
           the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those credit               致的信貸虧損（一項 12 個月預期信
           exposures for which there has been a significant increase           貸虧損）計提預期信貸虧損撥備。對
           in credit risk since initial recognition, a loss allowance is       於自首次確認以來信貸風險已大幅
           required for credit losses expected over the remaining life         增加的信貸敞口而言，則須就預期
           of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default (a       於敞口餘下年期內的信貸虧損計提
           lifetime ECL).                                                      虧損撥備，而不論違約時間（一項存
                                                                               續期預期信貸虧損）。

           At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether                  於各報告日期，本集團評估金融工
           the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased             具的信貸風險自首次確認以來有否
           significantly since initial recognition. When making the            大幅增加。在作出該項評估時，本
           assessment, the Group compares the risk of a default                集團將金融工具於報告日期發生違
           occurring on the financial instrument as at the reporting           約的風險與金融工具於首次確認日
           date with the risk of a default occurring on the financial          期發生違約的風險作出比較，並考
           instrument as at the date of initial recognition and considers      慮在毋須付出不必要成本或努力下
           reasonable and supportable information that is available            可得的合理及有根據資料，包括過
           without undue cost or effort, including historical and              往及前瞻性資料。當合約付款逾期
           forward-looking information. The Group considers that               超過90 日時，本集團認為信貸風險
           there has been a significant increase in credit risk when           已大幅增加。
           contractual payments are more than 90 days past due.

     (continued)
     Financial liabilities (continued)                                      金融負債（續）
     Subsequent measurement                                                 後續計量
     The subsequent measurement of financial liabilities depends            視乎金融負債的分類，金融負債的
     on their classification as follows:                                    後續計量方法如下：

     Financial liabilities at amortised cost (trade and other               按攤餘成本計算的金融負債（貿易及
     payables, and borrowings)                                              其他應付款項以及借貸）
     After initial recognition, trade and other payables, and               首次確認後，貿易及其他應付款項
     interest-bearing borrowings are subsequently measured                  以及計息借貸其後以實際利率法按
     at amortised cost, using the effective interest rate method            攤餘成本計量，倘折讓影響不大，
     unless the effect of discounting would be immaterial, in               則按成本列賬。倘負債終止確認及
     which case they are stated at cost. Gains and losses are               已透過實際利率攤銷，則收入及虧
     recognised in the statement of profit or loss when the                 損於損益表確認。
     liabilities are derecognised as well as through the effective
     interest rate amortisation process.


     Amortised cost is calculated by taking into account any                計算攤餘成本時已計及任何收購折
     discount or premium on acquisition and fees or costs                   讓或溢價，以及實際利率所包含的
     that are an integral part of the effective interest rate. The          費用或成本。實際利率攤銷計入損
     effective interest rate amortisation is included in finance            益表的財務成本。
     costs in the statement of profit or loss.


     Derecognition of financial liabilities                                 終止確認金融負債
     A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation under        金融負債於負債責任解除、取消或
     the liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.                  屆滿時終止確認。

     When an existing financial liability is replaced by another            倘現有金融負債被另一項由同一借
     from the same lender on substantially different terms, or the          貸人提供而絕大部分條款不同的負
     terms of an existing liability are substantially modified, such        債所取代，或現有負債的條款大幅
     an exchange or modification is treated as a derecognition of           修訂，則有關取代或修訂視作以終
     the original liability and a recognition of a new liability, and       止確認原有負債及確認新負債方式
     the difference between the respective carrying amounts is              處理，而相關賬面值的差額會於損
     recognised in the statement of profit or loss.                         益表內確認。

     When the contract contains a financing component which               一年的重大融資利益時，根據合約

           asset.

           that the economic benefits associated with the dividend will       本集團且股息的金額能夠可靠計
           flow to the Group and the amount of the dividend can be            量，則確認股息收入。

           receivables when the right to the consideration becomes            成為無條件時重新分類至貿易應收
           unconditional.                                                     款項。

           Contract liabilities                                               合約負債
           A contract liability is recognised when a payment is received      當本集團轉移相關貨品或服務前收
           or a payment is due (whichever is earlier) from a customer         取客戶付款或客戶付款到期（以較早
           before the Group transfers the related goods or services.          發生者為準）時，本集團便確認合約
           Contract liabilities are recognised as revenue when the            負債。當本集團根據合約履約（即向
           Group performs under the contract (i.e., transfers control of      客戶轉移相關貨品或服務的控制權）
           the related goods or services to the customer).                    時，合約負債確認為收入。

     (vii) Leases – Estimating the incremental borrowing                  (vii) 租賃 － 估計增量借款利率
         rate
         The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate                本集團無法輕易地釐定租賃內
         implicit in a lease, and therefore, it uses an incremental          含的利率，因此，其使用增量
         borrowing rate (“IBR”) to measure lease liabilities. The            借 款 利 率（「 增 量 借 款 利 率 」）
         IBR is the rate of interest that the Group would have               來計量租賃負債。增量借款利
         to pay to borrow over a similar term, and with a similar            率是本集團於類似經濟環境中
         security, the funds necessary to obtain an asset of                 為獲取與使用權資產價值相若
         a similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar              的資產，而以類似抵押品與類
         economic environment. The IBR therefore reflects                    似期限借入所需資金而需支付
         what the Group “would have to pay”, which requires                  的利息比率。因此，增量借款
         estimation when no observable rates are available                   利率反映本集團「需支付」的利
         (such as for subsidiaries that do not enter into                    率，當無可觀察的利率時（例
         financing transactions) or when it needs to be adjusted             如就並無訂立融資交易的附屬
         to reflect the terms and conditions of the lease (for               公司而言）或當需對利率作出
         example, when leases are not in the subsidiary’s                    調整以反映租賃的條款及條件
         functional currency). The Group estimates the IBR                   （例如租賃並非按附屬公司的
         using observable inputs (such as market interest rates)             功能貨幣計值）時，則須作出
         when available and is required to make certain entity-              利率估計。當可觀察輸入數據
         specific estimates (such as the subsidiary’s stand-                 （例如市場利率）可用時，本集
         alone credit rating).                                               團使用有關輸入數據來估計增
                                                                             量借款利率，並須作出若干實
                                                                             體特定的估計（例如附屬公司
                                                                             單獨的信貸評級）。

            consideration.

            The performance obligation is satisfied over time as                   履約責任隨著提供服務的時間

            normally required, except for tickets sold through travel              過旅行社出售的門票除外，其
            agencies, where payment is generally due within 30 to                  付款一般於交付門票起30 至
            90 days from the delivery of tickets.                                  90日到期。

            Provision of in-park recreation services                               提供園內遊樂服務
            The performance obligation is satisfied over time as                   履約責任隨著提供服務的時間
            services are rendered and payment by the customer                      滿足，一般客戶須於提供服務
            in advance before provision of services is normally                    前預先付款。
            required.

            The performance obligation is satisfied over time as                   履約責任隨著提供服務的時間

            required.


            Provision of consultancy, management and recreation                    提供諮詢、管理及遊樂服務
            services
            The performance obligation is satisfied over time                      履約責任隨著提供服務的時間
            as services are rendered and short-term advances                       滿足，提供服務前一般須預付
            are normally required before rendering the services.                   短期款項。管理服務合約為期
            Management service contracts are for periods of one                    一年或以下，並根據合約條款

           Under the income method, fair value is estimated using                 根據收入法，公允價值乃採用有關
           assumptions regarding the benefits and liabilities of                  資產壽命內所有權收益及負債的假
           ownership over the asset’s life including an exit or terminal          設進行估計（包括退出價值或終端價
           value. This method involves the projection of a series of              值）。該方法涉及對物業權益的一連
           cash flows on a property interest. A market-derived discount           串現金流量的預測。市場衍生的折
           rate is applied to the projected cash flow in order to                 現率適用於預測現金流量以便確立
           establish the present value of the income stream associated            與資產有關的收入流的現值。
           with the asset.

   The duration of the cash flows and the specific timing of              現金流量的持續時間及流入額和流

   reviews, lease renewal and related reletting, redevelopment            討、租約續簽及相關續租、重建或
   or refurbishment. The appropriate duration is driven by                翻新等事件決定。適當的持續時間
   market behaviour that is a characteristic of the class of              受 市 場 行 為（ 為 物 業 類 別 的 一 個 特
   property. The periodic cash flow is estimated as income                性 ）影 響 。 定 期 現 金 流 量 按 收 入 扣
   less non-recoverable expenses, collection losses, lease                除不可回收費用、收賬損失、租賃
   incentives, maintenance costs, agent and commission costs              獎勵、維修費用、代理和佣金費用
   and other operating and management expenses. The series                及其他經營和管理費用估算。該一
   of periodic net operating income, along with an estimate of            連串定期經營收入淨額，連同預計
   the terminal value anticipated at the end of the projection            於預測期終結時之終端價值估計金
   period, is then discounted.                                            額，折現至現值。

   A significant increase (decrease) in the market monthly rental        市場每月租金每年單獨大幅增加（減
   in isolation would result in a significant increase (decrease)        少）將導致投資物業的公允價值大幅
   in the fair value of the investment properties. A significant         增加（減少）。折現率單獨大幅增加
   increase (decrease) in the discount rate in isolation would           （減少）將導致投資物業的公允價值
   result in a significant decrease (increase) in the fair value of      大幅減少（增加）。
   the investment properties.

   and no ongoing payments will be made under the terms of                50 年，根據此等土地租賃的條款，
   these land leases. Leases of buildings generally have lease            將不會持續支付款項。樓宇租賃的
   terms between 2 and 10 years. Other equipment generally                租賃期一般為 2 至 10 年。其他設備
   has lease terms of 12 months or less and/or is individually of         的租賃期一般為12個月或以下及 ╱
   low value. Generally, the Group is restricted from assigning           或個別屬低價值。一般而言，本集
   and subleasing the leased assets outside the Group.                    團不可向本集團以外人士轉讓及分

   The Group is exposed to a number of risks related to its            本集團面對多種與其生物資產有關
   biological assets. The Group is exposed to the following            的風險。本集團面臨以下經營風
   operating risks:                                                    險：

   (i)    Regulatory and environmental risks                           (i)   監管及環境風險
          The Group is subject to laws and regulations in the                本集團須遵守其經營海獅、海
          location in which it operates breeding of sea lions,               豹及海豚繁殖所在地的法律及
          seals and dolphins. The Group has established                      規例。本集團已制定以符合當
          environmental policies and procedures aiming at                    地環境及其他法例為目標的環
          complying with local environmental and other laws.                 境政策及程序。管理層定期進

           Under the market method, fair value is estimated based on             根據市場法，公允價值乃根據市場
           the market-determined prices adjusted with reference to               釐定價格釐定，其按照物種、年
           the species, age, growing condition and cost incurred to              齡、成長情況及已產生成本作出調
           reflect differences in characteristics and/or stages of growth        整，以反映生物資產在成長特徵
           of biological assets less transaction costs to sell. Under the        及 ╱ 或階段方面的差異，並減去出
           income method, fair value is estimated using assumptions              售的交易成本。根據收入法，公允
           regarding the benefits and liabilities of ownership over              價值乃採用有關資產壽命內所有權
           the asset’s life including an exit or terminal value. This            收益及負債的假設進行估計（包括退
           method involves the projection of a series of cash flows on           出價值或終端價值）。該方法涉及對
           a biological asset interest. A market-derived discount rate           生物資產權益的一連串現金流量的
           is applied to the projected cash flow in order to establish           預測。市場衍生的折現率適用於預
           the present value of the income stream associated with the            測現金流量以便確立與資產有關的
           asset.                                                                收入流的現值。

      32. RESERVES                                                        32. 儲備
           The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements            本集團於本年度及以往年度的儲備
           therein for the current and prior years are presented in the     金額及其變動已載於合併權益變動
           consolidated statement of changes in equity.                     表。

           (a) Capital reserve                                              (a) 資本儲備
                The amounts represent deemed contributions from                該款項指視為權益持有人注資
                equity holders less deemed distributions to equity             減視為分派予權益持有人、本
                holders, considerations paid by the Group to non-              集團向非控股權益持有人就其
                controlling equity holders over their then share of net        當時分佔所收購附屬公司資產
                assets of subsidiaries acquired, and dilution upon             淨值支付代價及非控股權益持
                capital contributions by the non-controlling equity            有人注資時的攤薄。
                holders.


           (b) Statutory reserves                                           (b) 法定儲備
                In accordance with the PRC Company Law and                     根據中國公司法及中國附屬公
                the PRC subsidiaries’ articles of association, each            司的章程細則，在中國註冊為
                subsidiary registered in the PRC as a domestic                 內資公司的附屬公司各自須撥
                company is required to appropriate 10% of its annual           出 10% 的年度法定純利（經抵
                statutory net profit as determined under generally             銷任何往年虧損後）至法定盈
                accepted accounting principles of The People’s                 餘儲備。年度法定純利按中華
                Republic of China (“PRC GAAP”) (after offsetting any           人民共和國公認會計準則（「中
                prior years’ losses) to the statutory surplus reserves.        國會計準則」）釐定。倘該儲備
                When the balance of this reserve fund reaches 50%              金結餘達該實體資本的50%，
                of the entity’s capital, any further appropriation             則可選擇作出任何額外撥款。
                is optional. The statutory reserves can be utilised            法定儲備可用作抵銷往年虧損
                to offset prior years’ losses or to increase capital.          或增資。然而，作上述用途
                However, the balance of the statutory surplus reserves         後，該法定盈餘儲備結餘須維
                must be maintained at a minimum of 25% of the                  持為不少於資本的25%。
                capital after these usages.

           The Group’s finance department headed by the finance                以本集團財務經理為首的財務部門
           manager is responsible for determining the policies and             負責釐定金融工具公允價值計量的
           procedures for the fair value measurement of financial              政策及程序。財務經理直接向財務
           instruments. The finance manager reports directly to                總監及董事會報告。於各報告日
           the chief financial officer and the board of directors. At          期，財務部門分析金融工具的價值
           each reporting date, the finance department analyses                變動並釐定估值中適用的主要參
           the movements in the values of financial instruments and            數。估值由財務總監審核及批准。
           determines the major inputs applied in the valuation. The           估值過程及結果與董事會每年討論
           valuation is reviewed and approved by the chief financial           一次以進行年度財務申報。
           officer. The valuation process and results are discussed
           with the board of directors once a year for annual financial
           reporting.


           The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are         計入金融資產及金融負債的公允價
           included at the amount at which the instrument could be             值，乃自願訂約方於目前交易（受脅
           exchanged in a current transaction between willing parties,         迫或清盤出售交易除外）中可交換工
           other than in a forced or liquidation sale. The following           具的金額。估計公允價值時使用的
           methods and assumptions were used to estimate the fair              方法及假設如下：
           values:


           The fair values of the interest-bearing bank and other              按固定利率計息的計息銀行及其他
           borrowings with fixed interest rates have been calculated           借款的公允價值按適用於具有類似
           by discounting the expected future cash flows using rates           條款、信貸風險及剩餘年期的工具
           currently available for instruments with similar terms, credit      的現行利率折現預期未來現金流量
           risk and remaining maturities. The changes in fair value            計算。本集團本身於 2023 年 12 月
           as a result of the Group’s own non-performance risk for             31 日的計息銀行及其他借款的不履

           December 2023 were assessed to be insignificant.                    為不重大。

39. FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY                               39. 金 融 工 具 公 允 價 值 及 公 允
    OF FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS (continued)                                  價值等級（續）
   The fair values of unlisted equity investments designated at           指定為透過損益按公允價值列賬
   fair value through profit or loss have been estimated using            的非上市股本投資的公允價值，
   a market-based valuation technique based on assumptions                乃採用市場估值方法基於並無可
   that are not supported by observable market prices or                  觀察市場價格或比率支持的假設
   rates. The valuation requires the directors to determine               估計得出。進行估值時，董事須
   comparable public companies (peers) based on industry,                 根據行業、規模、槓桿及策略決
   size, leverage and strategy, and to calculate an appropriate           定 可 比 較 公 眾 公 司（ 同 業 ）， 以 及
   price multiple, such as enterprise value to earnings before            就每間已識別的可比較公司計算
   interest, taxes, depreciation and amortisation (“EV/EBITDA”)           相關價格倍數，例如企業價值相對
   multiple and price to earnings (“P/E”) multiple, for each              未計利息、稅項、折舊及攤銷前
   comparable company identified. The multiple is calculated              盈利（「E V / E B I T D A」）倍數及市盈
   by dividing the enterprise value of the comparable company            （「P/E」）率。該倍數乃按可比較公
   by an earnings measure. The trading multiple is then                   司的企業價值除以盈利的計量方法
   discounted for considerations such as illiquidity and size             計算得出。交易倍數因而就可比較
   differences between the comparable companies based on                  公司基於公司特定事實及情況的流
   company-specific facts and circumstances. The discounted               動性不足及規模差異等考慮因素作
   multiple is applied to the corresponding earnings measure              出折讓。折讓後的倍數應用於非上
   of the unlisted equity investments to measure the fair                 市股本投資的相關盈利計量，以計
   value. The directors believe that the estimated fair values            算公允價值。董事相信，因估值方
   resulting from the valuation technique, which are recorded             法產生的估計公允價值（於合併財務
   in the consolidated statement of financial position, and the           狀況表記賬）及相關的公允價值變動
   related changes in fair values, which are recorded in other           （於其他全面收益記賬）為合理，且
   comprehensive income, are reasonable, and that they were               於報告期末為最適當的價值。
   the most appropriate values at the end of the reporting
   period.


   The Group invests in an unlisted unit trust fund investment            本集團投資於透過損益按公允價值
   at fair value through profit or loss. The Group has estimated          列賬的非上市單位信託投資。本集
   the fair value of this unlisted investment by using a                  團已透過使用貼現現金流量估值模
   discounted cash flow valuation model based on the market               式，根據具有類似條款及風險的工
   interest rates of instruments with similar terms and risks.            具的市場利率估計此項非上市投資
                                                                          的公允價值。

   The Group invests in the equity of a company listed on                 本集團投資於一間在中國內地企業
   the over-the-counter exchange for companies in Chinese                 場外交易所掛牌的公司的股權。本
   Mainland. The Group has estimated the fair value of the                集團已透過使用二項樹模式估計該
   debt instrument by using the Binomial Tree Model.                      債務工具的公允價值。

   For the fair value of the financial assets at fair value through       就透過損益按公允價值列賬的金融

      40. F I N A N C I A L R I S K M A N A G E M E N T                     40. 財務風險管理目標及政策
          OBJECTIVES AND POLICIES
           The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise               本集團的主要金融工具包括計息銀
           interest-bearing bank and other borrowings, financial              行及其他借款、透過損益按公允價
           assets at fair value through profit or loss, pledged deposits      值列賬的金融資產、已抵押存款以
           and cash and bank balances. The main purpose of these              及現金及銀行結餘。此等金融工具
           financial instruments is to raise finance for the Group’s          主要用於為本集團籌集經營資金。
           operations. The Group has various other financial assets and       本集團有直接來自其運營的多種其
           liabilities such as trade receivables, trade payables, other       他金融資產及負債，例如貿易應收
           receivables and other payables, which arise directly from its      款項、貿易應付款項、其他應收款
           operations.                                                        項及其他應付款項。



           It is, and has been throughout the year under review, the          本集團的政策為及於回顧年度一直
           Group’s policy that no trading in financial instruments shall      為不進行金融工具交易。
           be undertaken.


           The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments      本集團的金融工具產生的主要風險
           are interest rate risk, foreign currency risk, credit risk and     為利率風險、外匯風險、信貸風險
           liquidity risk. The board of directors reviews and agrees          及流動資金風險。董事會檢討及協
           policies for managing each of these risks and they are             議控制各類風險的政策，有關政策
           summarised below.                                                  概述於下文。

      40. F I N A N C I A L R I S K M A N A G E M E N T                     40. 財 務 風 險 管 理 目 標 及 政 策
          OBJECTIVES AND POLICIES (continued)                                  （續）
           Capital management                                                  資本管理
           The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management            本集團資本管理的首要目標是維護
           are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going         本集團的持續經營能力及維持穩健
           concern and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to          的資本化比率，以支持其業務及為
           support its business and maximise equity holders’ value.            權益持有人帶來最大價值。

           The Group manages its capital structure and makes                   本集團根據經濟狀況變動及相關資
           adjustments to it in light of changes in economic conditions        產的風險特點管理及調整資本結
           and the risk characteristics of the underlying assets. To           構。為維持或調整資本結構，本集
           maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may             團或會調整派付予股東的股息、向
           adjust the dividend payment to shareholders, return capital         股東歸還資本或發行新股。本集
           to shareholders or issue new shares. The Group is not               團不受任何外部施加的資本要求
           subject to any externally imposed capital requirements. No          規限。於截至 2023 年 12 月 31 日及
           changes were made in the objectives, policies or processes          2022 年 12 月 31 日止年度，目標、
           during the years ended 31 December 2023 and 31                      政策或程序並無任何變動。
           December 2022.


           The Group monitors capital using a net debt to total                本集團利用淨負債總權益比率來監
           equity ratio. Net debt includes interest-bearing bank and           察資本。債務淨額包括計息銀行及
           other borrowings, and lease liabilities, less cash and cash         其他借貸以及租賃負債，減現金及
